    \"because\",\n    94→        \"about\",\n    95→        \"into\",\n    96→        \"through\",\n    97→        \"during\",\n    98→        \"before\",\n    99→        \"after\",\n   100→        \"above\",\n   101→        \"below\",\n   102→        \"between\",\n   103→        \"under\",\n   104→        \"again\",\n   105→        \"further\",\n   106→        \"once\",\n   107→        \"here\",\n   108→        \"there\",\n   109→        \"any\",\n   110→        \"do\",\n   111→        \"does\",\n   112→        \"did\",\n   113→        \"doing\",\n   114→        \"would\",\n   115→        \"could\",\n   116→        \"might\",\n   117→        \"must\",\n   118→        \"shall\",\n   119→        \"may\",\n   120→        \"been\",\n   121→        \"being\",\n   122→        \"am\",\n   123→    ]\n   124→)\n   125→\n   126→# Regex for tokenization\n   127→TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n   128→\n   129→\n   130→def tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n   131→    \"\"\"\n   132→    Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\n   133→\n   134→    Args:\n   135→        text: Input text string\n   136→\n   137→    Returns:\n   138→        List of lowercase tokens (words)\n   139→    \"\"\"\n   140→    # Find all words (2+ letters)\n   141→    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n   142→    # Filter stop words\n   143→    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n   144→\n   145→\n   146→def compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n   147→    \"\"\"\n   148→    Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\n   149→\n   150→    TF(t, d) = count(t in d) / total_terms(d)\n   151→\n   152→    Args:\n   153→        tokens: List of tokens from a document\n   154→\n   155→    Returns:\n   156→        Dictionary mapping terms to their normalized frequencies\n   157→    \"\"\"\n   158→    if not tokens:\n   159→        return {}\n   160→\n   161→    # Count raw frequencies\n   162→    counts = defaultdict(int)\n   163→    for token in tokens:\n   164→        counts[token] += 1\n   165→\n   166→    # Normalize by total token count\n   167→    total = len(tokens)\n   168→    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n   169→\n   170→\n   171→# ============================================================================\n   172→# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n   173→# ============================================================================\n   174→\n   175→\n   176→@dataclass\n   177→class TFIDFIndex:\n   178→    \"\"\"\n   179→    TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\n   180→\n   181→    Attributes:\n   182→        num_documents: Total number of documents indexed\n   183→        vocabulary: Set of all unique terms\n   184→        document_frequencies: term -> number of documents containing term\n   185→        idf: term -> inverse document frequency score\n   186→        inverted_index: term -> list of (doc_id, tf-idf score)\n   187→        doc_vectors: doc_id -> dict of term -> tf-idf score\n   188→        doc_norms: doc_id -> L2 norm of document vector\n   189→    \"\"\"\n   190→\n   191→    num_documents: int = 0\n   192→    vocabulary: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)\n   193→    document_frequencies: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n   194→    idf: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n   195→    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n   196→    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n   197→    doc_norms: dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n   198→\n   199→\n   200→@dataclass\n   201→class SearchResult:\n   202→    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n   203→\n   204→    doc_id: int\n   205→    score: float\n   206→    title: str = \"\"\n   207→\n   208→\n   209→@dataclass\n   210→class IndexingResult:\n   211→    \"\"\"Result from index building.\"\"\"\n   212→\n   213→    index: TFIDFIndex\n   214→    elapsed_time: float\n   215→    num_documents: int\n   216→    vocabulary_size: int\n   217→\n   218→\n   219→# ============================================================================\n   220→# Sequential Index Building\n   221→# ============================================================================\n   222→\n   223→\n   224→def build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents: list[Document]) -> IndexingResult:\n   225→    \"\"\"\n   226→    Build TF-IDF inverted index sequentially.\n   227→\n   228→    Steps:\n   229→    1. Tokenize all documents and compute term frequencies\n   230→    2. Calculate document frequencies for each term\n   231→    3. Compute IDF scores\n   232→    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n   233→    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n   234→\n   235→    Args:\n   236→        documents: List of Document objects to index\n   237→\n   238→    Returns:\n   239→        IndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n   240→    \"\"\"\n   241→    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n   242→\n   243→    index = TFIDFIndex()\n   244→    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n   245→\n   246→    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF\n   247→    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n   248→    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n   249→\n   250→    for doc in documents:\n   251→        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n   252→        tokens = tokenize(text)\n   253→        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n   254→        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n   255→        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n   256→        index.vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n   257→\n   258→    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n   259→    for term in index.vocabulary:\n   260→        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n   261→        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n   262→\n   263→    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n   264→    # IDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1 (smoothed)\n   265→    N = index.num_documents\n   266→    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n   267→        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n   268→\n   269→    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n   270→    for term in index.vocabulary:\n   271→        posting_list = []\n   272→        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n   273→            if term in tf_dict:\n   274→                tf = tf_dict[term]\n   275→                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n   276→                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n   277→        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n   278→        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n   279→        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n   280→\n   281→    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms\n   282→    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n   283→        doc_vector = {}\n   284→        norm_squared = 0.0\n   285→        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n   286→            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n   287→            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n   288→            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n   289→        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n   290→        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n   291→\n   292→    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n   293→\n   294→    return IndexingResult(index=index, elapsed_time=elapsed, num_documents=len(documents), vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary))\n   295→\n   296→\n   297→# ============================================================================\n   298→# Search Functions\n   299→# ============================================================================\n   300→\n   301→\n   302→def search_sequential(query: str, index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, documents: list[Document] = None) -> list[SearchResult]:  # noqa: RUF013\n   303→    \"\"\"\n   304→    Search for documents similar to query using cosine similarity.\n   305→\n   306→    Args:\n   307→        query: Search query string\n   308→        index: TF-IDF index to search\n   309→        top_k: Number of top results to return\n   310→        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n   311→\n   312→    Returns:\n   313→        List of SearchResult objects sorted by relevance\n   314→    \"\"\"\n   315→    # Tokenize query\n   316→    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n   317→    if not query_tokens:\n   318→        return []\n   319→\n   320→    # Compute query TF\n   321→    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n   322→\n   323→    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n   324→    query_vector = {}\n   325→    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n   326→    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n   327→        if term in index.idf:\n   328→            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n   329→            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n   330→            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n   331→\n   332→    if not query_vector:\n   333→        return []\n   334→\n   335→    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n   336→\n   337→    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n   338→    candidate_docs = set()\n   339→    for term in query_vector:\n   340→        if term in index.inverted_index:\n   341→            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n   342→                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n   343→\n   344→    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n   345→    scores = []\n   346→    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n   347→        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n   348→        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n   349→\n   350→        if doc_norm == 0:\n   351→            continue\n   352→\n   353→        # Dot product\n   354→        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n   355→\n   356→        # Cosine similarity\n   357→        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n   358→        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n   359→\n   360→    # Get top-k results\n   361→    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n   362→\n   363→    # Build result objects\n   364→    results = []\n   365→    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n   366→    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n   367→        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n   368→\n   369→    return results\n   370→\n   371→\n   372→def batch_search_sequential(\n   373→    queries: list[str],\n   374→    index: TFIDFIndex,\n   375→    top_k: int = 10,\n   376→    documents: list[Document] = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n   377→) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n   378→    \"\"\"\n   379→    Search for multiple queries sequentially.\n   380→\n   381→    Args:\n   382→        queries: List of search query strings\n   383→        index: TF-IDF index to search\n   384→        top_k: Number of top results per query\n   385→        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n   386→\n   387→    Returns:\n   388→        List of result lists, one per query\n   389→    \"\"\"\n   390→    return [search_sequential(query, index, top_k, documents) for query in queries]\n   391→\n   392→\n   393→# ============================================================================\n   394→# Main Entry Point\n   395→# ============================================================================\n   396→\n   397→\n   398→def main():\n   399→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Sequential TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n   400→    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n   401→    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n   402→    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n   403→    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n   404→\n   405→    args = parser.parse_args()\n   406→\n   407→    print(\"=\" * 60)\n   408→    print(\"Sequential TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n   409→    print(\"=\" * 60)\n   410→\n   411→    # Load or generate corpus\n   412→    if args.corpus:\n   413→        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n   414→        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n   415→    else:\n   416→        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n   417→        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n   418→\n   419→    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n   420→    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n   421→    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n   422→\n   423→    # Build index\n   424→    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index...\")\n   425→    result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n   426→\n   427→    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n   428→    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n   429→    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n   430→\n   431→    # Perform search\n   432→    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n   433→    print(\"-\" * 60)\n   434→\n   435→    search_start = time.perf_counter()\n   436→    results = search_sequential(args.query, result.index, args.top_k, documents)\n   437→    search_time = time.perf_counter() - search_start\n   438→\n   439→    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n   440→    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results)} results:\")\n   441→    for i, res in enumerate(results, 1):\n   442→        print(f\"  {i}. list[str], alpha: float = 1.2) -> str:\n   560→    \"\"\"\n   561→    Select an item using Zipf-like distribution.\n   562→    Earlier items in the list are more likely to be selected.\n   563→    \"\"\"\n   564→    n = len(items)\n   565→    weights = [1.0 / ((i + 1) ** alpha) for i in range(n)]\n   566→    return random.choices(items, weights=weights, k=1)[0]\n   567→\n   568→\n   569→def generate_sentence(topic_vocab: dict[str, list[str]], length: int = None) -> str:  # noqa: RUF013\n   570→    \"\"\"Generate a realistic-looking sentence.\"\"\"\n   571→    if length is None:\n   572→        length = random.randint(8, 25)\n   573→\n   574→    words = []\n   575→    general = TOPICS[\"general\"]\n   576→\n   577→    for i in range(length):  # noqa: B007\n   578→        roll = random.random()\n   579→\n   580→        if roll < 0.3:\n   581→            # Use connector/common word\n   582→            words.append(random.choice(CONNECTORS))\n   583→        elif roll < 0.55:\n   584→            # Use topic noun\n   585→            words.append(zipf_choice(topic_vocab[\"nouns\"]))\n   586→        elif roll < 0.75:\n   587→            # Use topic verb\n   588→            words.append(zipf_choice(topic_vocab[\"verbs\"]))\n   589→        elif roll < 0.85:\n   590→            # Use topic adjective\n   591→            words.append(zipf_choice(topic_vocab[\"adjectives\"]))\n   592→        elif roll < 0.92:\n   593→            # Use general noun\n   594→            words.append(zipf_choice(general[\"nouns\"]))\n   595→        else:\n   596→            # Use general verb/adjective\n   597→            if random.random() < 0.5:\n   598→                words.append(zipf_choice(general[\"verbs\"]))\n   599→            else:\n   600→                words.append(zipf_choice(general[\"adjectives\"]))\n   601→\n   602→    # Capitalize first word and add period\n   603→    if words:\n   604→        words[0] = words[0].capitalize()\n   605→        sentence = \" \".join(words) + \".\"\n   606→        return sentence\n   607→    return \"\"\n   608→\n   609→\n   610→def generate_paragraph(topic_vocab: dict[str, list[str]], sentences: int = None) -> str:  # noqa: RUF013\n   611→    \"\"\"Generate a paragraph with multiple sentences.\"\"\"\n   612→    if sentences is None:\n   613→        sentences = random.randint(3, 8)\n   614→\n   615→    return \" \".join(generate_sentence(topic_vocab) for _ in range(sentences))\n   616→\n   617→\n   618→def generate_document(doc_id: int, topic: str = None, min_words: int = 20, max_words: int = 10000) -> Document:  # noqa: RUF013\n   619→    \"\"\"\n   620→    Generate a single document with variable length.\n   621→\n   622→    Document lengths follow a log-normal distribution to simulate\n   623→    real-world variation (many short docs, few very long docs).\n   624→    High variance creates load balancing challenges for parallelization.\n   625→    \"\"\"\n   626→    if topic is None:\n   627→        topic = random.choice(list(TOPICS.keys()))\n   628→\n   629→    topic_vocab = TOPICS[topic]\n   630→\n   631→    # Log-normal distribution with HIGH variance for load imbalance\n   632→    # mu=5.5, sigma=1.5 creates range from ~20 to ~10000 words\n   633→    # This creates significant load balancing challenges\n   634→    target_words = int(random.lognormvariate(5.5, 1.5))\n   635→    target_words = max(min_words, min(max_words, target_words))\n   636→\n   637→    # Generate title\n   638→    title_words = [\n   639→        zipf_choice(topic_vocab[\"adjectives\"]).capitalize(),\n   640→        zipf_choice(topic_vocab[\"nouns\"]).capitalize(),\n   641→        random.choice([\"Analysis\", \"Study\", \"Overview\", \"Guide\", \"Review\", \"Introduction\", \"Exploration\", \"Investigation\"]),\n   642→    ]\n   643→    title = \" \".join(title_words)\n   644→\n   645→    # Generate content paragraphs until we reach target length\n   646→    paragraphs = []\n   647→    word_count = 0\n   648→\n   649→    while word_count < target_words:\n   650→        para = generate_paragraph(topic_vocab)\n   651→        paragraphs.append(para)\n   652→        word_count += len(para.split())\n   653→\n   654→    content = \"\\n\\n\".join(paragraphs)\n   655→\n   656→    return Document(doc_id=doc_id, title=title, content=content, topic=topic, word_count=len(content.split()))\n   657→\n   658→\n   659→def _generate_doc_worker(args: tuple[int, str, int, int, int]) -> Document:\n   660→    \"\"\"Worker function for parallel document generation.\"\"\"\n   661→    doc_id, topic, min_words, max_words, worker_seed = args\n   662→    random.seed(worker_seed)\n   663→    return generate_document(doc_id, topic, min_words, max_words)\n   664→\n   665→\n   666→def generate_corpus(\n   667→    num_docs: int,\n   668→    seed: int = 42,\n   669→    min_words: int = 50,\n   670→    max_words: int = 2000,\n   671→    topic_distribution: dict[str, float] = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n   672→    num_workers: int = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n   673→) -> list[Document]:\n   674→    \"\"\"\n   675→    Generate a corpus of documents in parallel.\n   676→\n   677→    Args:\n   678→        num_docs: Number of documents to generate\n   679→        seed: Random seed for reproducibility\n   680→        min_words: Minimum words per document\n   681→        max_words: Maximum words per document\n   682→        topic_distribution: Optional dict mapping topics to probabilities\n   683→        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n   684→    \"\"\"\n   685→\n   686→    random.seed(seed)\n   687→\n   688→    if topic_distribution is None:\n   689→        topics = list(TOPICS.keys())\n   690→        topic_distribution = {t: 1.0 / len(topics) for t in topics}\n   691→\n   692→    total = sum(topic_distribution.values())\n   693→    topic_probs = {t: p / total for t, p in topic_distribution.items()}\n   694→\n   695→    topics_list = list(topic_probs.keys())\n   696→    probs_list = list(topic_probs.values())\n   697→\n   698→    # Pre-generate all work items with unique seeds\n   699→    documents = []\n   700→    for i in range(num_docs):\n   701→        topic = random.choices(topics_list, weights=probs_list, k=1)[0]\n   702→        doc = generate_document(i, topic, min_words, max_words)\n   703→        documents.append(doc)\n   704→\n   705→    print(f\"Generated {len(documents)} documents.\")\n   706→    return documents\n   707→\n   708→\n   709→def save_corpus(documents: list[Document], filepath: str):\n   710→    \"\"\"Save corpus to JSON file.\"\"\"\n   711→    data = {\n   712→        \"metadata\": {\n   713→            \"num_documents\": len(documents),\n   714→            \"total_words\": sum(d.word_count for d in documents),\n   715→            \"topics\": list(set(d.topic for d in documents)),  # noqa: C401\n   716→        },\n   717→        \"documents\": [asdict(d) for d in documents],\n   718→    }\n   719→\n   720→    with open(filepath, \"w\") as f:\n   721→        json.dump(data, f, indent=2)\n   722→\n   723→    print(f\"Saved corpus to {filepath}\")\n   724→\n   725→\n   726→def load_corpus(filepath: str) -> list[Document]:\n   727→    \"\"\"Load corpus from JSON file.\"\"\"\n   728→    with open(filepath) I've now explored and read both Python files. Let me provide you with a comprehensive summary of the TF-IDF document search engine implementation.\n\n## Comprehensive Summary of TF-IDF Document Search Engine\n\n### 1. Codebase Structure\n\nThe `/root/workspace/` directory contains two main Python modules:\n\n**Files:**\n- `/root/workspace/sequential.py` - Sequential TF-IDF implementation (baseline)\n- `/root/workspace/document_generator.py` - Document corpus generator\n\n---\n\n### 2. Sequential Implementation Details\n\n#### **Text Processing (`sequential.py`)**\n\n**Tokenization & Stop Word Filtering:**\n- `STOP_WORDS`: A frozenset of 123 common English stop words (e.g., \"a\", \"an\", \"and\", \"the\", \"is\", etc.)\n- `TOKEN_PATTERN`: Regex pattern `r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\"` to extract words with 2+ letters\n- `tokenize()` function: Converts text to lowercase, extracts words matching the pattern, and filters out stop words\n\n**Term Frequency Computation:**\n- `compute_term_frequencies()`: Normalizes raw term counts by dividing by total token count\n- Formula: TF(t, d) = count(t in d) / total_terms(d)\n- Returns: Dictionary mapping terms to their normalized frequencies (0.0 to 1.0)\n\n---\n\n### 3. Data Structures\n\n#### **TFIDFIndex Dataclass** (lines 176-197)\n```python\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    num_documents: int                                    # Total indexed docs\n    vocabulary: set[str]                                  # All unique terms\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int]                  # df(term) = # docs containing term\n    idf: dict[str, float]                                 # log(N/df) + 1 for each term\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]]   # term -> [(doc_id, tfidf_score), ...]\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]]             # doc_id -> {term: tfidf_score}\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float]                          # doc_id -> L2 norm\n```\n\n**Key Fields:**\n- `inverted_index`: Posting lists sorted by TF-IDF score descending (for efficient top-k retrieval)\n- `doc_vectors`: Complete sparse vector representation for each document\n- `doc_norms`: Precomputed L2 norms for cosine similarity calculation\n\n#### **SearchResult Dataclass** (lines 200-206)\n```python\n@dataclass\nclass SearchResult:\n    doc_id: int\n    score: float\n    title: str = \"\"\n```\n\n#### **IndexingResult Dataclass** (lines 209-216)\n```python\n@dataclass\nclass IndexingResult:\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n```\n\n---\n\n### 4. Index Building Process\n\n**Function:** `build_tfidf_index_sequential()` (lines 224-294)\n\n**Five-Step Algorithm:**\n\n**Step 1: Tokenization & Term Frequency (lines 246-256)**\n- Concatenates document title and content\n- Tokenizes combined text\n- Computes TF for each document\n- Builds vocabulary by collecting all unique terms\n\n**Step 2: Document Frequency Calculation (lines 258-261)**\n- For each term in vocabulary, counts how many documents contain it\n- `df(term) = count of documents containing the term`\n\n**Step 3: IDF Computation (lines 263-267)**\n- Formula: IDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1\n- Where N = total number of documents\n- The +1 smoothing prevents zero scores\n\n**Step 4: Inverted Index Construction (lines 269-279)**\n- For each term, creates a posting list of (doc_id, tfidf_score) tuples\n- TF-IDF score: tf * idf\n- **Sorts by score descending** for efficient top-k retrieval\n\n**Step 5: Document Vectors & Norms (lines 281-290)**\n- Builds complete sparse vector representation for each document\n- For each term in a document: stores term → tfidf_score mapping\n- Computes L2 norm: sqrt(sum of tfidf_score²) for cosine similarity normalization\n\n**Time Complexity:** O(D*T + T*D + D*T) where D = documents, T = average terms per document\n\n---\n\n### 5. Search Functions\n\n#### **Single Query Search: `search_sequential()` (lines 302-369)**\n\n**Algorithm:**\n1. **Query Tokenization**: Tokenizes query string, returns empty if no valid terms\n2. **Query TF Computation**: Computes term frequencies for query\n3. **Query Vector Building**: \n   - Creates sparse vector with term → tfidf_score\n   - Only includes terms that exist in the index\n   - Computes query norm for normalization\n4. **Candidate Collection**: \n   - Finds all documents containing at least one query term\n   - Uses inverted index for efficient lookup\n5. **Similarity Calculation**:\n   - For each candidate document, computes cosine similarity:\n   - Formula: similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n   - Dot product: sum of (query_tfidf * doc_tfidf) for matching terms\n6. **Top-K Selection**: Uses `heapq.nlargest()` to efficiently get top-k results\n\n**Returns:** List of `SearchResult` objects with doc_id, score, and title\n\n#### **Batch Query Search: `batch_search_sequential()` (lines 372-390)**\n- Simple sequential wrapper that calls `search_sequential()` for each query\n- Returns list of result lists\n\n---\n\n### 6. Document Generator (`document_generator.py`)\n\n#### **Document Dataclass** (lines 548-556)\n```python\n@dataclass\nclass Document:\n    doc_id: int\n    title: str\n    content: str\n    topic: str\n    word_count: int\n```\n\n#### **Topic-Specific Vocabularies**\n\nFive topic domains with realistic word distributions:\n- **technology**: algorithm, software, hardware, database, machine learning, neural, docker, etc.\n- **science**: research, hypothesis, experiment, analysis, particle, molecule, etc.\n- **business**: market, customer, revenue, profit, strategy, investment, etc.\n- **health**: patient, diagnosis, treatment, therapy, medication, physician, etc.\n- **general**: common words across all domains\n\nEach topic has three word categories: nouns, verbs, adjectives\n\n#### **Document Generation**\n\n**Zipf Distribution:** `zipf_choice()` implements Zipf-like probability for realistic word frequency distribution\n\n**Content Generation:**\n- `generate_sentence()`: Random length (8-25 words), mixed vocabulary and connectors\n- `generate_paragraph()`: Multiple sentences (3-8)\n- `generate_document()`: \n  - **Log-normal document length** (μ=5.5, σ=1.5): Creates high variance (20-10,000 words)\n  - This variance intentionally creates load balancing challenges for parallelization\n  - Generates realistic-looking title and multi-paragraph content\n\n**Corpus Generation:**\n- `generate_corpus()`: Generates multiple documents with topic distribution\n- Default: 5000-10000 documents with variable length\n- Reproducible with random seed\n\n#### **I/O Functions:**\n- `save_corpus()`: Saves documents to JSON with metadata\n- `load_corpus()`: Loads documents from JSON file\n\n---\n\n### 7. Key Algorithmic Characteristics\n\n| Aspect | Details |\n|--------|---------|\n| **Search Algorithm** | Cosine similarity with TF-IDF weighting |\n| **Index Type** | Inverted index with posting lists |\n| **Posting List Optimization** | Sorted by TF-IDF score (descending) |\n| **Candidate Selection** | Union of documents from all query term posting lists |\n| **Similarity Metric** | Cosine: dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm) |\n| **Top-K Retrieval** | `heapq.nlargest()` for efficiency |\n| **Term Weighting** | TF (normalized) × IDF (log) |\n| **Preprocessing** | Lowercasing, stop word removal, 2+ letter filter |\n\n---\n\n### 8. Performance Characteristics\n\n**Index Building:**\n- **First pass**: O(D) tokenization + O(T) frequency computation\n- **Second pass**: O(T*D) document frequency calculation\n- **Third pass**: O(T) IDF computation\n- **Fourth pass**: O(T*D) inverted index construction with sorting\n- **Fifth pass**: O(D*T) document vector building\n- Overall: O(D*T + T*D*log(D)) due to sorting\n\n**Search:**\n- **Query processing**: O(Q) tokenization + O(Q*log(vocab))\n- **Candidate collection**: O(|Q terms| * avg_posting_list_size)\n- **Similarity computation**: O(candidates * |query terms|)\n- **Top-K selection**: O(candidates * log(k))\n\n---\n\n### 9. Main Entry Points\n\n**`sequential.py main()`**: Demonstrates complete workflow\n- Loads/generates corpus\n- Builds TF-IDF index\n- Executes single query search\n- Reports timing and results\n\n**`document_generator.py main()`**: Corpus generation utility\n- Generates corpus with configurable parameters\n- Saves to JSON\n- Prints statistics\n\n---\n\nThis is a well-structured, classical TF-IDF implementation suitable as a baseline for performance comparisons with parallel implementations. \"\"\"\n   226→    Build TF-IDF inverted index sequentially.\n   227→\n   228→    Steps:\n   229→    1. Compute query TF-IDF vector\n   324→    query_vector = {}\n   325→    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n   326→    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n   327→        if term in index.idf:\n   328→            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n   329→            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n   330→            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n   331→\n   332→    if not query_vector:\n   333→        return []\n   334→\n   335→    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n   336→\n   337→    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n   338→    candidate_docs = set()\n   339→    for term in query_vector:\n   340→        if term in index.inverted_index:\n   341→            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n   342→                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n   343→\n   344→    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n   345→    scores = []\n   346→    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n   347→        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n   348→        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n   349→\n   350→        if doc_norm == 0:\n   351→            continue\n   352→\n   353→        # Dot product\n   354→        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n   355→\n   356→        # Cosine similarity\n   357→        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n   358→        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n   359→\n   360→    # Get top-k results\n   361→    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n   362→\n   363→    # Build result objects\n   364→    results = []\n   365→    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n   366→    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n   367→        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n   368→\n   369→    return results\n   370→\n   371→\n   372→def batch_search_sequential(\n   373→    queries: list[str],\n   374→    index: TFIDFIndex,\n   375→    top_k: int = 10,\n   376→    documents: list[Document] = None,  #        Dictionary mapping terms to their normalized frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    # Count raw frequencies\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass IndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Sequential Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents: list[Document]) -> IndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index sequentially.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Tokenize all documents and compute term frequencies\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for each term\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n\n    Returns:\n        IndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Tokenize documents and compute TF\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        index.vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    # IDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1 (smoothed)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return IndexingResult(index=index, elapsed_time=elapsed, num_documents=len(documents), vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary))\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef search_sequential(query: str, index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, documents: list[Document] = None) -> list[SearchResult]:  # noqa: RUF013\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for documents similar to query using cosine similarity.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects sorted by relevance\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_sequential(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries sequentially.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists, one per query\n    \"\"\"\n    return [search_sequential(query, index, top_k, documents) for query in queries]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Sequential TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Sequential TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    search_start = time.perf_counter()\n    results = search_sequential(args.query, result.index, args.top_k, documents)\n    search_time = time.perf_counter() - search_start\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results)} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results, 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","numLines":449,"startLine":1,"totalLines":449}}}

 \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents: tokenize and compute TF.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_chunk: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (doc_id, term_freqs, terms, idf, doc_term_freqs = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(args: Tuple[List[int], Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[str, float]]) -> Dict[int, Tuple[Dict[str, float], float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and norms for a subset of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_id_list, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id to (vector, norm)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    result = {}\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n    return result\n\n\ndef _chunk_list(lst: list, num_chunks: int) -> List[list]:\n    \"\"\"Split a list into approximately equal chunks.\"\"\"\n    if num_chunks <= 0:\n        return [lst]\n    chunk_size = max(1, len(lst) // num_chunks)\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(lst), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(lst[i:i + chunk_size])\n    # Merge small last chunk if it's much smaller\n    if len(chunks) > num_chunks and len(chunks[-1]) < chunk_size // 2:\n        last = chunks.pop()\n        chunks[-1].extend(last)\n    return chunks\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: Document vector and norm computation\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Approximate size of document chunks for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures (compatible with sequential)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(docs_chunk: List[Tuple[int, str, str]]) -> List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], Set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents: tokenize and compute TF.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_chunk: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (doc_id, term_freqs, document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = index entries for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (term_list, idf_dict, doc_term_freqs)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to posting lists\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, idf, doc_term_freqs = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(args: Tuple[List[int], Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[str, float]]) -> Dict[int, Tuple[Dict[str, float], float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and norms for a subset of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_id_list, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id to (vector, norm)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    result = {}\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n    return result\n\n\ndef _chunk_list(lst: list, num_chunks: int) -> List[list]:\n    \"\"\"Split a list into approximately equal chunks.\"\"\"\n    if num_chunks <= 0:\n        return [lst]\n    chunk_size = max(1, len(lst) // num_chunks)\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(lst), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(lst[i:i + chunk_size])\n    # Merge small last chunk if it's much smaller\n    if len(chunks) > num_chunks and len(chunks[-1]) < chunk_size // 2:\n        last = chunks.pop()\n        chunks[-1].extend(last)\n    return chunks\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") if doc_titles else f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[List[Document]] = None\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build title lookup dict\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else None\n\n    # Prepare search arguments\n    search_args = [(query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n\n    # Execute searches in parallel\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        results = list(executor.map(_search_single_query, search_args))\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test parallel implementation against sequential.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Top K results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Check IDF values (with floating point tolerance)\n    for term in seq_idx.idf:\n        assert abs(seq_idx.idf[term] - par_idx.idf[term]) < 1e-10, f\"IDF mismatch for {term}\"\n\n    print(\"✓ Index correctness verified\")\n\n    # re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n\ndef tokenize(text: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures (compatible with sequential)\n# ============================================================================\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _process_document_batch(doc_batch: List[Tuple[int, str, str]]) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute TF.\n    Returns aggregated results to reduce merge overhead.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocab = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in doc_batch:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocab\n\n\ndef _compute_df_batch(args: Tuple[List[str], List[Set[str]]]) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a batch of terms.\n    Uses list of term sets for faster iteration.\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, doc_term_sets = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for term_set in doc_term_sets if term in term_set)\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_batch(args: Tuple[List[str], Dict[str, float], List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]]]) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index entries for a batch of terms.\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, idf, doc_tf_list = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_list:\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf_dict[term] * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_batch(args: Tuple[List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]], Dict[str, float]]) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and norms for a batch of documents.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_tf_list, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_list:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _chunk_list(lst: list, num_chunks: int) -> List[list]:\n    \"\"\"Split a list into approximately equal chunks.\"\"\"\n    if num_chunks <= 0 or not lst:\n        return [lst] if lst else []\n    chunk_size = max(1, (len(lst) + num_chunks - 1) // num_chunks)\n    return [lst[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(lst), chunk_size)]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing to parallelize:\n    1. Document tokenization and TF computation\n    2. Document frequency calculation\n    3. Inverted index construction\n    4. Document vector and norm computation\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Approximate size of document chunks for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n\n    # Prepare document data for parallel processing\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Dynamic chunk sizing: ensure enough chunks for parallelism\n    num_doc_chunks = max(num_workers * 2, num_docs // chunk_size + 1)\n    doc_chunks = _chunk_list(doc_data, num_doc_chunks)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        batch_results = list(executor.map(_process_document_batch, doc_chunks))\n\n    # Merge results\n    for batch_tf, batch_terms, batch_vocab in batch_results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(batch_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(batch_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(batch_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Parallel document frequency calculation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunks = _chunk_list(vocab_list, num_workers * 4)\n    doc_term_sets = list(doc_terms.values())  # Convert to list for faster iteration\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        df_args = [(chunk, doc_term_sets) for chunk in term_chunks]\n        df_results = list(executor.map(_compute_df_batch, df_args))\n\n    for df_chunk in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_chunk)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast O(V))\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Parallel inverted index construction\n    doc_tf_list = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        inv_args = [(chunk, index.idf, doc_tf_list) for chunk in term_chunks]\n        inv_results = list(executor.map(_build_inverted_index_batch, inv_args))\n\n    for inv_chunk in inv_results:\n        index.inverted_index.update(inv_chunk)\n\n    # Step 5: Parallel document vector and norm computation\n    doc_tf_chunks = _chunk_list(doc_tf_list, num_workers * 2)\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        vec_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_tf_chunks]\n        vec_results = list(executor.map(_build_doc_vectors_batch, vec_args))\n\n    for vec_dict, norm_dict in vec_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(vec_dict)\n        index.doc_norms.update(norm_dict)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search - Using module-level globals for worker processes\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for search workers (set during initialization)\n_search_index = None\n_search_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index_data: Dict[str, Any], doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize search worker with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n\n    # Reconstruct the index from dict data\n    _search_index = TFIDFIndex(\n        num_documents=index_data['num_documents'],\n        vocabulary=index_data['vocabulary'],\n        document_frequencies=index_data['document_frequencies'],\n        idf=index_data['idf'],\n        inverted_index=index_data['inverted_index'],\n        doc_vectors=index_data['doc_vectors'],\n        doc_norms=index_data['doc_norms']\n    )\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(query_batch: List[Tuple[int, str, int]]) -> List[Tuple[int, List[SearchResult]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in a batch using the global index.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n    for query_idx, query, top_k in query_batch:\n        query_results = _search_single(query, _search_index, top_k, _search_doc_titles)\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single(query: str, index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int, doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Execute a single search query.\"\"\"\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") if doc_titles else f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[List[Document]] = None\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else None\n\n    # Convert index to serializable dict (done once)\n    index_data = {\n        'num_documents': index.num_documents,\n        'vocabulary': index.vocabulary,\n        'document_frequencies': index.document_frequencies,\n        'idf': index.idf,\n        'inverted_index': index.inverted_index,\n        'doc_vectors': index.doc_vectors,\n        'doc_norms': index.doc_norms\n    }\n\n    # Prepare query batches with indices to maintain order\n    query_data = [(i, q, top_k) for i, q in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Split into larger batches to reduce overhead\n    batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 2))\n    query_batches = _chunk_list(query_data, len(queries) // batch_size + 1)\n\n    # Execute searches in parallel with initializer\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(\n        max_workers=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index_data, doc_titles)\n    ) as executor:\n        batch_results = list(executor.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches))\n\n    # Merge and sort results by original query index\n    all_results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        all_results.extend(batch)\n\n    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    results = [r[1] for r in all_results]\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n#        return []\n   343→\n   344→    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n   345→\n   346→    candidate_docs = set()\n   347→    for term in query_vector:\n   348→        if term in TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures (compatible with sequential)\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _process_document_batch(doc_batch: List[Tuple[int, str, str]]) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute TF.\n    Returns aggregated results to reduce merge overhead.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocab = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in doc_batch:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocab\n\n\ndef _compute_df_batch(args: Tuple[List[str], List[Set[str]]]) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a batch of terms.\n    Uses list of term sets for faster iteration.\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, doc_term_sets = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for term_set in doc_term_sets if term in term_set)\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_batch(args: Tuple[List[str], Dict[str, float], List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]]]) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index entries for a batch of terms.\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, idf, doc_tf_list = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_list:\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf_dict[term] * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_batch(args: Tuple[List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]], Dict[str, float]]) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and norms for a batch of documents.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_tf_list, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_list:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _chunk_list(lst: list, num_chunks: int) -> List[list]:\n    \"\"\"Split a list into approximately equal chunks.\"\"\"\n    if num_chunks <= 0 or not lst:\n        return [lst] if lst else []\n    chunk_size = max(1, (len(lst) + num_chunks - 1) // num_chunks)\n    return [lst[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(lst), chunk_size)]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing to parallelize:\n    1. Document tokenization and TF computation\n    2. Document frequency calculation\n    3. Inverted index construction\n    4. Document vector and norm computation\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Approximate size of document chunks for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n\n    # Prepare document data for parallel processing\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Dynamic chunk sizing: ensure enough chunks for parallelism\n    num_doc_chunks = max(num_workers * 2, num_docs // chunk_size + 1)\n    doc_chunks = _chunk_list(doc_data, num_doc_chunks)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        batch_results = list(executor.map(_process_document_batch, doc_chunks))\n\n    # Merge results\n    for batch_tf, batch_terms, batch_vocab in batch_results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(batch_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(batch_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(batch_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Parallel document frequency calculation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunks = _chunk_list(vocab_list, num_workers * 4)\n    doc_term_sets = list(doc_terms.values())  # Convert to list for faster iteration\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        df_args = [(chunk, doc_term_sets) for chunk in term_chunks]\n        df_results = list(executor.map(_compute_df_batch, df_args))\n\n    for df_chunk in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_chunk)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast O(V))\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Parallel inverted index construction\n    doc_tf_list = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        inv_args = [(chunk, index.idf, doc_tf_list) for chunk in term_chunks]\n        inv_results = list(executor.map(_build_inverted_index_batch, inv_args))\n\n    for inv_chunk in inv_results:\n        index.inverted_index.update(inv_chunk)\n\n    # Step 5: Parallel document vector and norm computation\n    doc_tf_chunks = _chunk_list(doc_tf_list, num_workers * 2)\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        vec_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_tf_chunks]\n        vec_results = list(executor.map(_build_doc_vectors_batch, vec_args))\n\n    for vec_dict, norm_dict in vec_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(vec_dict)\n        index.doc_norms.update(norm_dict)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search - Using module-level globals for worker processes\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for search workers (set during initialization)\n_search_index = None\n_search_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index_data: Dict[str, Any], doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize search worker with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n\n    # Reconstruct the index from dict data\n    _search_index = TFIDFIndex(\n        num_documents=index_data['num_documents'],\n        vocabulary=index_data['vocabulary'],\n        document_frequencies=index_data['document_frequencies'],\n        idf=index_data['idf'],\n        inverted_index=index_data['inverted_index'],\n        doc_vectors=index_data['doc_vectors'],\n        doc_norms=index_data['doc_norms']\n    )\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(query_batch: List[Tuple[int, str, int]]) -> List[Tuple[int, List[SearchResult]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in a batch using the global index.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n    for query_idx, query, top_k in query_batch:\n        query_results = _search_single(query, _search_index, top_k, _search_doc_titles)\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single(query: str, index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int, doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Execute a single search query.\"\"\"\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") if doc_titles else f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[List[Document]] = None\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term
