{term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures (compatible with sequential)\n# ============================================================================\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = 0\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)\n    document_frequencies: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    idf: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    inverted_index: Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_vectors: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_norms: Dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n    doc_id: int\n    score: float\n    title: str = \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helper Functions (for multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(docs_chunk: List[Tuple[int, str, str]]) -> List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], Set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents: tokenize and compute TF.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_chunk: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (doc_id, term_freqs, term_set) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, title, content in docs_chunk:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        results.append((doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())))\n    return results\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(args: Tuple[List[str], Dict[int, Set[str]]]) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (term_list, doc_terms_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_chunk(args: Tuple[List[str], Dict[str, float], Dict[int, Dict[str, float]]]) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index entries for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (term_list, idf_dict, doc_term_freqs)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to posting lists\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, idf, doc_term_freqs = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(args: Tuple[List[int], Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[str, float]]) -> Dict[int, Tuple[Dict[str, float], float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and norms for a subset of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_id_list, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id to (vector, norm)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    result = {}\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        result[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n    return result\n\n\ndef _chunk_list(lst: list, num_chunks: int) -> List[list]:\n    \"\"\"Split a list into approximately equal chunks.\"\"\"\n    if num_chunks <= 0:\n        return [lst]\n    chunk_size = max(1, len(lst) // num_chunks)\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(lst), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(lst[i:i + chunk_size])\n    # Merge small last chunk if it's much smaller\n    if len(chunks) > num_chunks and len(chunks[-1]) < chunk_size // 2:\n        last = chunks.pop()\n        chunks[-1].extend(last)\n    return chunks\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing to parallelize:\n    1. Document tokenization and TF computation\n    2. Document frequency calculation\n    3. Inverted index construction\n    4. Document vector and norm computation\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Approximate size of document chunks for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Prepare document data for parallel processing\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Calculate chunk size based on num_workers\n    actual_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 4))\n    doc_chunks = _chunk_list(doc_data, len(documents) // actual_chunk_size + 1)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        chunk_results = list(executor.map(_process_document_chunk, doc_chunks))\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    for chunk_result in chunk_results:\n        for doc_id, tf, terms in chunk_result:\n            doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n            doc_terms[doc_id] = terms\n            index.vocabulary.update(terms)\n\n    # Step 2: Parallel document frequency calculation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunks = _chunk_list(vocab_list, num_workers)\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        df_args = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in term_chunks]\n        df_results = list(executor.map(_compute_df_for_terms, df_args))\n\n    # Merge DF results\n    for df_chunk in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_chunk)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Parallel inverted index construction\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        inv_args = [(chunk, index.idf, doc_term_freqs) for chunk in term_chunks]\n        inv_results = list(executor.map(_build_inverted_index_chunk, inv_args))\n\n    # Merge inverted index results\n    for inv_chunk in inv_results:\n        index.inverted_index.update(inv_chunk)\n\n    # Step 5: Parallel document vector and norm computation\n    doc_id_list = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunks = _chunk_list(doc_id_list, num_workers)\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        vec_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, index.idf) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n        vec_results = list(executor.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, vec_args))\n\n    # Merge document vector results\n    for vec_chunk in vec_results:\n        for doc_id, (vec, norm) in vec_chunk.items():\n            index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = vec\n            index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _search_single_query(args: Tuple[str, TFIDFIndex, int, Optional[Dict[int, str]]]) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for documents similar to a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") if doc_titles else f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[List[Document]] = None\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n\n    # Build index - Sequential\n    print(\"\\n--- Index Building ---\")\n    print(\"Building sequential index...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Verify index correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Index Correctness Check ---\")\n    seq_idx = seq_result.index\n    par_idx = par_result.index\n\n    assert seq_idx.num_documents == par_idx.num_documents, \"Document count mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.vocabulary == par_idx.vocabulary, \"Vocabulary mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.document_frequencies == par_idx.document_frequencies, \"DF mismatch\"\n\n    # ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing Pool for efficient parallel processing with reduced\n    serialization    chunk_size: Approximate size of document chunks for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n\n    # Prepare with imap_unordered for better performance\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n\n    # Calculate optimal chunksize for imap\n    # This is parallelized by term since vocabulary can be large\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    doc_term_sets = list(doc_terms.values())\n\n    def compute_df_for_term(term: str) -> Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast O(V))\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # posting_list = []\n        term_idf = index.idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_items:\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf_dict[term] * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        return term, posting_list\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        return doc_id, doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        vec_chunksize = max(1, num_docs // (num_workers * 8))\n        for doc_id, doc_vector, norm in pool.imap_unordered(build_doc_vector, doc_tf_items, doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures (compatible with sequential)\n# ============================================================================\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = 0\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)\n    document_frequencies: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    idf: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    inverted_index: Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_vectors: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_norms: Dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n    doc_id: int\n    score: float\n    title: str = \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _process_document_batch(doc_batch: List[Tuple[int, str, str]]) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute TF.\n    Returns aggregated results to reduce merge overhead.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocab = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in doc_batch:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocab.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocab\n\n\ndef _compute_df_batch(args: Tuple[List[str], List[Set[str]]]) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a batch of terms.\n    Uses list of term sets for faster iteration.\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, doc_term_sets = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for term_set in doc_term_sets if term in term_set)\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_inverted_index_batch(args: Tuple[List[str], Dict[str, float], List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]]]) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index entries for a batch of terms.\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, idf, doc_tf_list = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_list:\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf_dict[term] * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        result[term] = posting_list\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_batch(args: Tuple[List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]], Dict[str, float]]) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and norms for a batch of documents.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_tf_list, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_list:\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _chunk_list(lst: list, num_chunks: int) -> List[list]:\n    \"\"\"Split a list into approximately equal chunks.\"\"\"\n    if num_chunks <= 0 or not lst:\n        return [lst] if lst else []\n    chunk_size = max(1, (len(lst) + num_chunks - 1) // num_chunks)\n    return [lst[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(lst), chunk_size)]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing to parallelize:\n    1. Document tokenization and TF computation\n    2. Document frequency calculation\n    3. Inverted index construction\n    4. Document vector and norm computation\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Approximate size of document chunks for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n\n    # Prepare document data for parallel processing\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Dynamic chunk sizing: ensure enough chunks for parallelism\n    num_doc_chunks = max(num_workers * 2, num_docs // chunk_size + 1)\n    doc_chunks = _chunk_list(doc_data, num_doc_chunks)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        batch_results = list(executor.map(_process_document_batch, doc_chunks))\n\n    # Merge results\n    for batch_tf, batch_terms, batch_vocab in batch_results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(batch_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(batch_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(batch_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Parallel document frequency calculation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunks = _chunk_list(vocab_list, num_workers * 4)\n    doc_term_sets = list(doc_terms.values())  # Convert to list for faster iteration\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        df_args = [(chunk, doc_term_sets) for chunk in term_chunks]\n        df_results = list(executor.map(_compute_df_batch, df_args))\n\n    for df_chunk in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_chunk)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast O(V))\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Parallel inverted index construction\n    doc_tf_list = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        inv_args = [(chunk, index.idf, doc_tf_list) for chunk in term_chunks]\n        inv_results = list(executor.map(_build_inverted_index_batch, inv_args))\n\n    for inv_chunk in inv_results:\n        index.inverted_index.update(inv_chunk)\n\n    # Step 5: Parallel document vector and norm computation\n    doc_tf_chunks = _chunk_list(doc_tf_list, num_workers * 2)\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        vec_args = [(chunk, index.idf) for chunk in doc_tf_chunks]\n        vec_results = list(executor.map(_build_doc_vectors_batch, vec_args))\n\n    for vec_dict, norm_dict in vec_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(vec_dict)\n        index.doc_norms.update(norm_dict)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search - Using module-level globals for worker processes\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for search workers (set during initialization)\n_search_index = None\n_search_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index_data: Dict[str, Any], doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize search worker with shared index data.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n\n    # Reconstruct the index from dict data\n    _search_index = TFIDFIndex(\n        num_documents=index_data['num_documents'],\n        vocabulary=index_data['vocabulary'],\n        document_frequencies=index_data['document_frequencies'],\n        idf=index_data['idf'],\n        inverted_index=index_data['inverted_index'],\n        doc_vectors=index_data['doc_vectors'],\n        doc_norms=index_data['doc_norms']\n    )\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(query_batch: List[Tuple[int, str, int]]) -> List[Tuple[int, List[SearchResult]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in a batch using the global index.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n    for query_idx, query, top_k in query_batch:\n        query_results = _search_single(query, _search_index, top_k, _search_doc_titles)\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_single(query: str, index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int, doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Execute a single search query.\"\"\"\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") if doc_titles else f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[List[Document]] = None\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else None\n\n    # Convert index to serializable dict (done once)\n    index_data = {\n        'num_documents': index.num_documents,\n        'vocabulary': index.vocabulary,\n        'document_frequencies': index.document_frequencies,\n        'idf': index.idf,\n        'inverted_index': index.inverted_index,\n        'doc_vectors': index.doc_vectors,\n        'doc_norms': index.doc_norms\n    }\n\n    # Prepare query batches with indices to maintain order\n    query_data = [(i, q, top_k) for i, q in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Split into larger batches to reduce overhead\n    batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 2))\n    query_batches = _chunk_list(query_data, len(queries) // batch_size + 1)\n\n    # Execute searches in parallel with initializer\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(\n        max_workers=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index_data, doc_titles)\n    ) as executor:\n        batch_results = list(executor.map(_search_query_batch, query_batches))\n\n    # Merge and sort results by original query index\n    all_results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        all_results.extend(batch)\n\n    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    results = [r[1] for r in all_results]\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test parallel implementation against sequential.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Top K results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n\n    # Build index - Sequential\n    print(\"\\n--- Index Building ---\")\n    print(\"Building sequential index...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Build index - Parallel\n    print(f\"Building parallel index ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n    print(f\"Parallel: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    index_speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n    print(f\"Index Speedup: {index_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify index correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Index Correctness Check ---\")\n search\n    print(\"Running sequential search...\")\n    seq_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_results = batch_search_sequential(test_queries, seq_idx, args.top_k, documents)\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential: {seq_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Parallel search\n    print(f\"Running parallel search ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_results, seq_search_time / par_search_time\n    print(f\"Search Speedup: {search_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify search correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Search Correctness Check ---\")\n    for i, (seq_res,           assert s.doc_id == p.doc_id, f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Doc ID mismatch\"\n            assert abs(s.score - p.score) < 1e-10, f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Score mismatch\"\n\n    print(\"✓ _idf_global\n    doc_id, tf_dict = args\n    doc_vector = {}\n    norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n        tfidf = tf * _idf_global[term]\n        doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing Pool for efficient parallel processing with\n    initializers to share - very fast O(V))\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index (parallelized with initializer)\n    doc_tf_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_inv_worker, initargs=(doc_tf_items, index.idf)) as pool:\n   269→def _init_search_worker(index_data: Dict[str, Any], doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]):\n   270→    \"\"\"Initialize search worker with index data.\"\"\"\n   271→    global _search_index, _search_single_query(args: Tuple[int, str, int]) -> Tuple[int, List[SearchResult]]:\n   285→    \"\"\"Search for a single query using global index.\"\"\"\n   286→    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n   287→\n   288→    query_idx, query, top_k = args\n   289→    index = _search_index\n   290→    doc_titles = _search_doc_titles\n   291→\n   292→    query_tokens = 299→    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n   300→    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n   301→        if term in index.idf:\n   302→            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n   303→ index.inverted_index[term]:\n   315→                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n   316→\n   317→    scores = []\n   318→    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n   319→        doc_vector = = sum(\n   326→            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n   327→            for term in query_vector\n   328→        )\n   329→\n   330→        similarity = dot_product / _idf_global\n    doc_id, tf_dict = args\n    doc_vector = {}\n    norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n        tfidf = tf * _idf_global[term]\n        doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n        norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n    return doc_id, doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building - Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _process_single_document(args: Tuple[int, str, str]) -> Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Process a single document: tokenize and compute TF.\"\"\"\n    doc_id, title, content = args\n    text = title + \" \" + content\n    tokens = tokenize(text)\n    tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n    return doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing Pool for efficient parallel processing with\n    initializers to share large data structures.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Approximate size of document chunks for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n\n    # Prepare document data - use tuples for efficient pickling\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n\n    pool_chunksize = max(1, num_docs // (num_workers * 8))\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        for doc_id, tf, terms in pool.imap_unordered(_process_single_document, doc_data, chunksize=pool_chunksize):\n            doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n            doc_terms[doc_id] = terms\n            index.vocabulary.update(terms)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies (parallelized with initializer)\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    doc_term_sets = list(doc_terms.values())\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_df_worker, initargs=(doc_term_sets,)) as pool:\n        df_chunksize = max(1, len(vocab_list) // (num_workers * 8))\n        for term, df in pool.imap_unordered(_compute_df_for_term, vocab_list, chunksize=df_chunksize):\n            index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast O(V))\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index (parallelized with initializer)\n    doc_tf_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_inv_worker, initargs=(doc_tf_items, index.idf)) as pool:\n        inv_chunksize = max(1, len(vocab_list) // (num_workers * 8))\n        for term, posting_list in pool.imap_unordered(_build_posting_list, vocab_list, chunksize=inv_chunksize):\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and norms (parallelized with initializer)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_vec_worker, initargs=(index.idf,)) as pool:\n        vec_chunksize = max(1, num_docs // (num_workers * 8))\n        for doc_id, doc_vector, norm in pool.imap_unordered(_build_doc_vector, doc_tf_items, chunksize=vec_chunksize):\n            index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index_data: Dict[str, Any], doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize search worker with index data.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    _search_index = TFIDFIndex(\n        num_documents=index_data['num_documents'],\n        vocabulary=index_data['vocabulary'],\n        document_frequencies=index_data['document_frequencies'],\n        idf=index_data['idf'],\n        inverted_index=index_data['inverted_index'],\n        doc_vectors=index_data['doc_vectors'],\n        doc_norms=index_data['doc_norms']\n    )\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args: Tuple[int, str, int]) -> Tuple[int, List[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query using global index.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n\n    query_idx, query, top_k = args\n    index = _search_index\n    doc_titles = _search_doc_titles\n\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return query_idx, []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return query_idx, []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") if doc_titles else f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return query_idx, results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[List[Document]] = None\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses Pool with initializer for efficient index sharing.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else None\n\n    # Convert index to dict for serialization\n    index_data = {\n        'num_documents': index.num_documents,\n        'vocabulary': index.vocabulary,\n        'document_frequencies': index.document_frequencies,\n        'idf': index.idf,\n        'inverted_index': index.inverted_index,\n        'doc_vectors': index.doc_vectors,\n        'doc_norms': index.doc_norms\n    }\n\n    # Prepare query data\n    query_data = [(i, q, top_k) for i, q in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index_data, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        chunksize = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 4))\n        results_unordered = list(pool.imap_unordered(_search_single_query, query_data, chunksize=chunksize))\n\n    # Sort by query index to restore order\n    results_unordered.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    results = [r[1] for r in results_unordered]\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main       \"have\", \"had\", \"what\", \"when\", \"where\", \"who\", \"which\", \"why\",\n        \"how\", \"all\", \"each\", \"every\", \"both\", \"few\", \"more\", \"most\",\n        \"other\", TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures (compatible with sequential)\n# ============================================================================\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = 0\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)\n    document_frequencies: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    idf: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    inverted_index: Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_vectors: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_norms: Dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n    doc_id: int\n    score: float\n    title: str = \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building - Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _process_single_document(args: Tuple[int, str, str]) -> Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Process a single document: tokenize and compute TF.\"\"\"\n    doc_id, title, content = args\n    text = title + \" \" + content\n    tokens = tokenize(text)\n    tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n    return doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())\n\n\ndef _process_document_batch(doc_batch: List[Tuple[int, str, str]]) -> List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], Set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"Process a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n    return [_process_single_document(doc) for doc in doc_batch]\n\n\ndef _chunk_list(lst: list, num_chunks: int) -> List[list]:\n    \"\"\"Split a list into approximately equal chunks.\"\"\"\n    if num_chunks <= 0 or not lst:\n        return [lst] if lst else []\n    chunk_size = max(1, (len(lst) + num_chunks - 1) // num_chunks)\n    return [lst[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(lst), chunk_size)]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing Pool for efficient parallel processing with reduced\n    serialization overhead using imap_unordered and optimal chunk sizes.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Approximate size of document chunks for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = num_docs\n\n    # Prepare document data - use tuples for efficient pickling\n    doc_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # Use Pool with imap_unordered for better performance\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n\n    # Calculate optimal chunksize for imap\n    pool_chunksize = max(1, num_docs // (num_workers * 8))\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        for doc_id, tf, terms in pool.imap_unordered(_process_single_document, doc_data, chunksize=pool_chunksize):\n            doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n            doc_terms[doc_id] = terms\n            index.vocabulary.update(terms)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    # This is parallelized by term since vocabulary can be large\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    doc_term_sets = list(doc_terms.values())\n\n    def compute_df_for_term(term: str) -> Tuple[str, int]:\n        df = sum(1 for term_set in doc_term_sets if term in term_set)\n        return term, df\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_chunksize = max(1, len(vocab_list) // (num_workers * 8))\n        for term, df in pool.imap_unordered(compute_df_for_term, vocab_list, chunksize=df_chunksize):\n            index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast O(V))\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index\n    # Parallelize by term\n    doc_tf_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    def build_posting_list(term: str) -> Tuple[str, List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n        posting_list = []\n        term_idf = index.idf[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_items:\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf_dict[term] * term_idf\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        return term, posting_list\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        inv_chunksize = max(1, len(vocab_list) // (num_workers * 8))\n        for term, posting_list in pool.imap_unordered(build_posting_list, vocab_list, chunksize=inv_chunksize):\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and norms\n    # Parallelize by document\n    idf_dict = index.idf\n\n    def build_doc_vector(args: Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]) -> Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], float]:\n        doc_id, tf_dict = args\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        return doc_id, doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        vec_chunksize = max(1, num_docs // (num_workers * 8))\n        for doc_id, doc_vector, norm in pool.imap_unordered(build_doc_vector, doc_tf_items, chunksize=vec_chunksize):\n            index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n# Global variables for search workers\n_search_index = None\n_search_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index_data: Dict[str, Any], doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize search worker with index data.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    _search_index = TFIDFIndex(\n        num_documents=index_data['num_documents'],\n        vocabulary=index_data['vocabulary'],\n        document_frequencies=index_data['document_frequencies'],\n        idf=index_data['idf'],\n        inverted_index=index_data['inverted_index'],\n        doc_vectors=index_data['doc_vectors'],\n        doc_norms=index_data['doc_norms']\n    )\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args: Tuple[int, str, int]) -> Tuple[int, List[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query using global index.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n\n    query_idx, query, top_k = args\n    index = _search_index\n    doc_titles = _search_doc_titles\n\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return query_idx, []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return query_idx, []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") if doc_titles else f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return query_idx, results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[List[Document]] = None\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses Pool with initializer for efficient index sharing.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else None\n\n    # Convert index to dict for serialization\n    index_data = {\n        'num_documents': index.num_documents,\n        'vocabulary': index.vocabulary,\n        'document_frequencies': index.document_frequencies,\n        'idf': index.idf,\n        'inverted_index': index.inverted_index,\n        'doc_vectors': index.doc_vectors,\n        'doc_norms': index.doc_norms\n    }\n\n    # Prepare query data\n    query_data = [(i, q, top_k) for i, q in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index_data, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        chunksize = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 4))\n        results_unordered = list(pool.imap_unordered(_search_single_query, query_data, chunksize=chunksize))\n\n    # Sort by query index to restore order\n    results_unordered.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    results = [r[1] for r in results_unordered]\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test parallel implementation against sequential.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Top K results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n\n    # Build index - Sequential\n    print(\"\\n--- Index Building ---\")\n    print(\"Building sequential index...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Build index - Parallel\n    print(f\"Building parallel index ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n    print(f\"Parallel: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    index_speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n    print(f\"Index Speedup: {index_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify index correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Index Correctness Check ---\")\n    seq_idx = seq_result.index\n    par_idx = par_result.index\n\n    assert seq_idx.num_documents == par_idx.num_documents, \"Document count mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.vocabulary == par_idx.vocabulary, \"Vocabulary mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.document_frequencies == par_idx.document_frequencies, \"DF mismatch\"\n\n    for term in seq_idx.idf:\n        assert abs(seq_idx.idf[term] - par_idx.idf[term]) < 1e-10, f\"IDF mismatch for {term}\"\n\n    print(\"✓ Index correctness verified\")\n\n    # Generate test queries\n    print(f\"\\n--- Search Performance ({args.num_queries} queries) ---\")\n    test_queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm neural network\",\n        \"data analysis pipeline optimization\",\n        \"cloud computing infrastructure scalability\",\n        \"security authentication encryption protocol\",\n        \"distributed system architecture design\",\n    ] * (args.num_queries // 5)\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(\"Running sequential search...\")\n    seq_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_results = batch_search_sequential(test_queries, seq_idx, args.top_k, documents)\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential: {seq_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Parallel search\n    print(f\"Running parallel search ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_results, par_search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        test_queries, par_idx, args.top_k, args.num_workers, documents\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel: {par_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    search_speedup = seq_search_time / par_search_time\n    print(f\"Search Speedup: {search_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify search correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Search Correctness Check ---\")\n    for i, (seq_res, par_res) in enumerate(zip(seq_results, par_results)):\n        assert len(seq_res) == len(par_res), f\"Query {i}: Result count mismatch\"\n        for j, (s, p) in enumerate(zip(seq_res, par_res)):\n            assert s.doc_id == p.doc_id, f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Doc ID mismatch\"\n            assert abs(s.score - p.score) < 1e-10, f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Score mismatch\"\n\n    print(\"✓ Search correctness verified\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Summary:\")\n    print(f\"  Index Building Speedup: {index_speedup:.2f}x (target: 1.5x)\")\n    print(f\"  Search Speedup: {search_speedup:.2f}x (target: 2.0x)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}

\"for\", \"from\",\n        \"has\", \"he\", \"in\", \"is\", \"it\", \"its\", \"of\", \"on\", \"that\", \"the\",\n        \"to\", \"was\", \"were\", \"will\", \"with\", \"the\", \"this\", \"but\", \"other\", \"some\", \"such\", \"no\", \"not\", \"only\", \"same\", \"so\",\n        \"than\", \"too\", \"very\", \"just\", \"can\", \"should\", \"now\", \"or\",\n        \"if\", \"then\", TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures (compatible with sequential)\n# ============================================================================\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = inverted_index: Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_vectors: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_norms: Dict[int, float] = ============================================================================\n# Module-level globals for multiprocessing workers\n# ============================================================================\n\n# For document frequency computation\n_doc_term_sets_global: List[Set[str]] = []\n\n# For inverted index building\n_doc_tf_items_global: List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]] = []\n_idf_global: Dict[str, float] = {}\n\n# For search workers\n_search_index = None\n_search_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_df_worker(doc_term_sets: List[Set[str]]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for document frequency computation.\"\"\"\n    global _doc_term_sets_global\n    _doc_term_sets_global = doc_term_sets\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_term(term: str) -> Tuple[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequency for a single term.\"\"\"\n    global _doc_term_sets_global\n    df = sum(1 for term_set in _doc_term_sets_global if term in term_set)\n    return term, df\n\n\ndef _init_inv_worker(doc_tf_items: List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]], idf: Dict[str, float]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"Build posting list for a single term.\"\"\"\n    global _doc_tf_items_global, _idf_global\n    posting_list = []\n    term_idf = _idf_global[term]\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in _doc_tf_items_global:\n        if term in tf_dict:\n            tfidf = tf_dict[term] * term_idf\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n    posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], = idf\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vector(args: Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]) -> Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], float]:\n    \"\"\"Build document vector and norm for a single document.\"\"\"\n    global _idf_global\n    doc_id, tf_dict = args\n    doc_vector = {}\n    norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n        tfidf = tf * _idf_global[term]\n        doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n        norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n    return doc_id, doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building - Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _process_single_document(args: Tuple[int, str, str]) -> Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n    return doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing Pool for efficient parallel processing with\n    initializers to share - very fast O(V))\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index (parallelized with initializer)\n    doc_tf_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_inv_worker, initargs=(doc_tf_items, index.idf)) as pool:\n index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and norms (parallelized with initializer)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_vec_worker, _init_search_worker(index_data: Dict[str, Any], doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize search worker with index data.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: in _doc_term_sets_global if term in term_set)\n    return term, df\n\n\ndef _init_inv_worker(doc_tf_items: List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]], idf: Dict[str, float]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for = idf\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vector(args: Tuple[int, Dict[str, float]]) -> Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], float]:\n    \"\"\"Build document vector and norm for a single document.\"\"\"\n    global _idf_global\n    doc_id, tf_dict = args\n    doc_vector = {}\n    norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n        tfidf = tf * _idf_global[term]\n        doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n Building - Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _process_single_document(args: Tuple[int, str, str]) -> Tuple[int, Dict[str, float], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Process a single document: tokenize and compute TF.\"\"\"\n    doc_id, title, content = args\n    text = title + \" \" + content\n    tokens = tokenize(text)\n    tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n    return doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Uses multiprocessing Pool for efficient parallel processing with\n    initializers to share large data structures.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Approximate size of document chunks for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is index.vocabulary.update(terms)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies (parallelized with initializer)\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    doc_term_sets = list(doc_terms.values())\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_df_worker, initargs=(doc_term_sets,)) as pool:\n        df_chunksize = max(1, len(vocab_list) // (num_workers * 8))\n        for term, df in pool.imap_unordered(_compute_df_for_term, vocab_list, chunksize=df_chunksize):\n            index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast O(V))\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index (parallelized with initializer)\n    doc_tf_items = list(doc_term_freqs.items())\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_inv_worker, initargs=(doc_tf_items, index.idf)) as pool:\n        inv_chunksize = max(1, len(vocab_list) // (num_workers * 8))\n        for term, posting_list in pool.imap_unordered(_build_posting_list, vocab_list, chunksize=inv_chunksize):\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and norms (parallelized with initializer)\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_vec_worker, initargs=(index.idf,)) as pool:\n        vec_chunksize = max(1, num_docs // (num_workers * 8))\n        for doc_id, doc_vector, norm in pool.imap_unordered(_build_doc_vector, doc_tf_items, ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index_data: Dict[str, Any], doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize search worker with index data.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    _search_index = TFIDFIndex(\n        num_documents=index_data['num_documents'],\n        vocabulary=index_data['vocabulary'],\n        document_frequencies=index_data['document_frequencies'],\n        idf=index_data['idf'],\n        inverted_index=index_data['inverted_index'],\n        doc_vectors=index_data['doc_vectors'],\n        doc_norms=index_data['doc_norms']\n    )\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_impl(query: str, index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int, doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Internal implementation of single query search.\"\"\"\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") if doc_titles else f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(batch: List[Tuple[int, str, int]]) -> List[Tuple[int, List[SearchResult]]]:\n    \"\"\"Search a batch of queries using global index.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    results = []\n    for query_idx, {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures (compatible with sequential)\n# search.\"\"\"\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") tfidf\n   162→        norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n   163→    return doc_id, doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n   164→\n   165→\n   166→# _search_single_impl(query: str, index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int, doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]) -> List[SearchResult]:\n   285→    \"\"\"Internal implementation of single query search.\"\"\"\n   286→    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n   287→    if not query_tokens:\n   288→        return []\n   289→\n   290→    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n   291→\n   292→ results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n   333→\n   334→    return results\n   335→\n   336→\n   337→def _search_query_batch(batch: List[Tuple[int, str, int]]) ->
