ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=num_docs,\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index_data: Dict[str, Any], doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]):\n    \"\"\"Initialize search worker with index data.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    _search_index = TFIDFIndex(\n        num_documents=index_data['num_documents'],\n        vocabulary=index_data['vocabulary'],\n        document_frequencies=index_data['document_frequencies'],\n        idf=index_data['idf'],\n        inverted_index=index_data['inverted_index'],\n        doc_vectors=index_data['doc_vectors'],\n        doc_norms=index_data['doc_norms']\n    )\n    _search_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\ndef _search_single_impl(query: str, index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int, doc_titles: Optional[Dict[int, str]]) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"Internal implementation of single query search.\"\"\"\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\") if doc_titles else f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(batch: List[Tuple[int, str, int]]) -> List[Tuple[int, List[SearchResult]]]:\n    \"\"\"Search a batch of queries using global index.\"\"\"\n    global _search_index, _search_doc_titles\n    results = []\n    for query_idx, query, top_k in batch:\n        query_results = _search_single_impl(query, _search_index, top_k, _search_doc_titles)\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n    return results\n\n\ndef _chunk_list(lst: list, num_chunks: int) -> List[list]:\n    \"\"\"Split a list into approximately equal chunks.\"\"\"\n    if num_chunks <= 0 or not lst:\n        return [lst] if lst else []\n    chunk_size = max(1, (len(lst) + num_chunks - 1) // num_chunks)\n    return [lst[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(lst), chunk_size)]\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[List[Document]] = None\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses Pool with initializer for efficient index sharing. Batches queries\n    to reduce inter-process communication overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (defaults to CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else None\n\n    # Convert index to dict for serialization - only include fields needed for search\n    # Exclude vocabulary and document_frequencies as they're not needed for search\n    index_data = {\n        'num_documents': index.num_documents,\n        'vocabulary': set(),  # Not needed for search\n        'document_frequencies': {},  # Not needed for search\n        'idf': index.idf,\n        'inverted_index': index.inverted_index,\n        'doc_vectors': index.doc_vectors,\n        'doc_norms': index.doc_norms\n    }\n\n    # Prepare query data with indices\n    query_data = [(i, q, top_k) for i, q in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Split into batches - one batch per worker for minimal IPC\n    # Each worker processes multiple queries\n    batches = _chunk_list(query_data, num_workers)\n\n    # Use Pool with initializer - map over batches\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index_data, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch, batches)\n\n    # Flatten and sort by query index to restore order\n    all_results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        all_results.extend(batch)\n\n    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    results = [r[1] for r in all_results]\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test parallel implementation against sequential.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-queries\", type=int, default=100, help=\"Number of queries\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Top K results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Generate corpus\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n\n    # Build index - Sequential\n    print(\"\\n--- Index Building ---\")\n    print(\"Building sequential index...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Build index - Parallel\n    print(f\"Building parallel index ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n    print(f\"Parallel: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    index_speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n    print(f\"Index Speedup: {index_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify index correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Index Correctness Check ---\")\n    seq_idx = seq_result.index\n    par_idx = par_result.index\n\n    assert seq_idx.num_documents == par_idx.num_documents, \"Document count mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.vocabulary == par_idx.vocabulary, \"Vocabulary mismatch\"\n    assert seq_idx.document_frequencies == par_idx.document_frequencies, \"DF mismatch\"\n\n    for term in seq_idx.idf:\n        assert abs(seq_idx.idf[term] - par_idx.idf[term]) < 1e-10, f\"IDF mismatch for {term}\"\n\n    print(\"✓ Index correctness verified\")\n\n    # Generate test queries\n    print(f\"\\n--- Search Performance ({args.num_queries} queries) ---\")\n    test_queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm neural network\",\n        \"data analysis pipeline optimization\",\n        \"cloud computing infrastructure scalability\",\n        \"security authentication encryption protocol\",\n        \"distributed system architecture design\",\n    ] * (args.num_queries // 5)\n\n    # Sequential search\n    print(\"Running sequential search...\")\n    seq_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_results = batch_search_sequential(test_queries, seq_idx, args.top_k, documents)\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential: {seq_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    # Parallel search\n    print(f\"Running parallel search ({args.num_workers} workers)...\")\n    par_results, par_search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        test_queries, par_idx, args.top_k, args.num_workers, documents\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel: {par_search_time:.3f}s\")\n\n    search_speedup = seq_search_time / par_search_time\n    print(f\"Search Speedup: {search_speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify search correctness\n    print(\"\\n--- Search Correctness Check ---\")\n    for i, (seq_res, par_res) in enumerate(zip(seq_results, par_results)):\n        assert len(seq_res) == len(par_res), f\"Query {i}: Result count mismatch\"\n        for j, (s, p) in enumerate(zip(seq_res, par_res)):\n            assert s.doc_id == p.doc_id, f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Doc ID mismatch\"\n            assert abs(s.score - p.score) < 1e-10, f\"Query {i}, Result {j}: Score mismatch\"\n\n    print(\"✓ Search correctness verified\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Summary:\")\n    print(f\"  Index Building Speedup: {index_speedup:.2f}x (target: 1.5x)\")\n    print(f\"  Search Speedup: {search_speedup:.2f}x (target: 2.0x)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","numLines":526,"startLine":1,"totalLines":526}}}
